公司簡介
COMPANY PROFILE
農夫山泉股份有限公司成立於1996年，為 Established in 1996, Nongfu Springs Co., Ltd. is a leader in the
中國包裝飲用水及飲料的龍頭企業。我們的 packaged drinking water and beverage industry in the PRC. Our
產品主要覆蓋包裝飲用水、茶飲料、功能飲 major products include packaged drinking water, tea beverage,
料及果汁飲料等類別。本公司於2001年6月 functional beverage and juice beverage. The Company was
從有限責任公司整體變更為股份有限公司， transformed from a limited liability company into a joint stock
並於2020年9月在香港聯交所主板上市（股 company with limited liabilities in June 2001 and listed on the Main
份代號：9633. HK）。 Board of the Hong Kong Stock Exchange in September 2020 (stock
code: 9633. HK).
我們秉持「天然、健康」的產品理念，堅持使 We believe in offering natural and healthy products and insist on
用優質原料。我們的包裝飲用水全部源自優 using high quality raw materials. All of our packaged drinking water
質天然水源，含天然的礦物元素。我們擁有 products are sourced from quality natural water sources containing
領先的裝備水平、製造能力、強大的供應鏈 natural mineral elements. We possess industry-leading equipment,
管理能力和嚴格的質量保障體系。 manufacturing capabilities, strong supply chain management
capabilities and rigorous quality assurance systems.
我們建立了包裝飲用水和飲料的雙引擎發展 We have established dual growth engines, packaged drinking water
格局，擁有覆蓋各省市及縣級行政區域的全 and beverage and set up a nationwide sales network covering all
國性銷售網絡。我們實行多品牌戰略，具有 provinces, municipalities and prefecture-level administrative divisions
長遠的品牌規劃和強大的品牌傳播能力，鑄 across China. We implement a multi-brand strategy, have long-term
就了中國軟飲料的超級品牌。 visions for our brands and strong brand communication capabilities,
and have built a super brand in the PRC soft beverage market.
2
農夫山泉股份有限公司 NONGFU SPRING CO., LTD. 年報 2021 Annual Report

董事長致辭
CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT

在可見未來有足夠財務資源繼續經營。因此 the Group has adequate financial resources to continue its operation

製。 prepared on a going concern basis.

則》及公司章程）的相關規定外，亦同時考 rules (including but not limited to the Company Law, the Listing
慮諸多多元化因素，包括但不限於性別、年 Rules and the Articles of Association), the Company should also
齡、文化及教育背景、專業經驗、技能、知 consider various diversity factors, including but not limited to
識及╱或服務年限。提名委員會會按董事人 gender, age, culture and education background, professional

化政策亦包含監察及彙報機制和檢討機制， shall make final recommendation on the appointment to the Board

策，從性別、年齡、文化及教育背景、專業 includes monitoring, reporting and reviewing system to ensure the
經驗、技能、知識及╱或服務年限而言，董 effectiveness and successful implementation of the policy. The

風險管理及內部監控 RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL
董事會已按照《企業管治守則》第二部分第 The Board has established a risk management and internal control
D.2段的要求建立了風險管理及內部監控系 system in accordance with the requirements of paragraph D.2 under
統，負責對本公司的風險管理及內部監控系 Section II of the Corporate Governance Code, which is responsible
統的充分性和有效性進行獨立審核，持續監 for independently reviewing the adequacy and effectiveness of the
督並每年至少檢討一次其運行的有效性。該 risk management and internal control system of the Company,
系統旨在管理而非消除未能達成業務目標的 and continuously monitoring and reviewing the effectiveness of its
風險，促進有效及高效運營，合理保證財務 operation once a year. The system aims to manage rather than
報告可靠性及遵守適用法律及法規以及保障 eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business objectives, promote
本集團資產。董事會只能就不會有重大的失 effective and efficient operations, reasonably ensure the reliability of
實陳述或損失作出合理而非絕對的保證。 financial reports and comply with applicable laws and regulations
and protect the assets of the Group. The Board can only give
reasonable but not absolute assurance that there will be no material
misrepresentation or loss.

主要業務 PRINCIPAL BUSINESS
本集團是中國包裝飲用水及飲料的龍頭企 The Group is a leader in the packaged drinking water and beverage
業，致力於向消費者提供高質量的產品。本 business in the PRC with devotion to providing consumers with high-
集團的主要產品覆蓋包裝飲用水、茶飲料、 quality products. The Group’s major products include packaged
功能飲料及果汁飲料等類別。 drinking water, tea beverage, functional beverage and juice
beverage.
自1996年成立以來，本集團即確保「每一滴 Since its establishment in 1996, the Group has adhered to the
農夫山泉都有它的源頭」。本集團的包裝飲 principle that “every drop of Nongfu Spring has its source” 「( 每一
用水產品全部源自天然水源。我們已在中國 滴農夫山泉都有它的源頭」). The Group sources all of packaged
各不同區域佈局了十一大優質水源地，全面 drinking water products from natural water sources. We have
覆蓋了全國市場供應。該十一大水源所在地 obtained access to ten quality natural water sources in different
生態環境卓越，水質優異。本集團對優質天 regions of China which cover the entire national market supply. The
然原水做包括過濾及殺菌在內的必要處理。 ten water sources are all located in excellent ecological environments
我們的包裝飲用水產品含有鉀、鈉、鈣、 and have outstanding water quality. The Group treats the quality
鎂、偏矽酸等對人體有益的礦物元素。 natural water with the necessary process, including filtration
and sterilization. Our packaged drinking water products contain
the natural minerals that are beneficial to human body, such as
potassium, sodium, calcium, magnesium and metasilicate.

不獲全面豁免持續關連交易的豁免申請 Waiver Application for Non-fully Exempt Continuing
Connected Transactions
根據《上市規則》，鍾先生及養生堂為本公司 Mr. Zhong and Yangshengtang are substantial Shareholders of the
的主要股東，屬本公司的關連人士。因此， Company and the connected persons of the Company under the
上述交易構成本公司的持續關連交易。 Listing Rules. Therefore, the transactions abovementioned constitute
the continuing connected transactions of the Company.
由於上述不獲全面豁免持續關連交易預期將 As the above non-fully exempt continuing connected transactions
按經常性及持續基準繼續進行，董事認為， are expected to continue a recurring and continuing basis, our
遵守上述公告規定乃不切實際，將使我們招 Directors consider that compliance with the above announcement
致不必要的行政費用，並對我們造成不必要 requirements will be impractical, will incur unnecessary administrative
的負擔。 costs for us, and will be unduly burdensome to us.
因此，我們已向香港聯交所申請，而香港聯 Accordingly, we have applied to the Hong Kong Stock Exchange
交所根據《上市規則》第14A.105條已授予我 for, and the Hong Kong Stock Exchange has granted, a waiver to
們就上述不獲豁免持續關連交易遵守公告規 us under Rule 14A.105 of the Listing Rules from compliance with
定的豁免。 the announcement requirements in respect of the above non-fully
exempt continuing connected transactions.
本公司確認上述截至2021年12月31日止年 The Company has confirmed that the execution and enforcement
度的持續關連交易項下具體協議的簽訂及執 of the implementation agreements under the continuing connected
行均已遵循該等持續關連交易的定價原則。 transactions set above for the year ended December 31, 2021
has followed the pricing principles of such continuing connected
transactions.

董事已審閱以上不獲全面豁免的持續關連交 non-executive Directors had reviewed the above non-fully exempt

環境政策及表現 ENVIRONMENTAL POLICY AND PERFORMANCE
本集團遵守《中華人民共和國環境保護法》、 The Group complies with relevant environmental protection laws
《中華人民共和國環境影響評價法》、《中華 and regulations, including the Law on Environmental Protection
人民共和國水法》、《建設項目環境保護管理 of the People’s Republic of China, the Environmental Impact
條例》等環境保護相關法律法規。 Assessment Law of the People’s Republic of China, the Water
Law of the People’s Republic of China, and the Regulations on the
Environmental Protection Management of Construction Projects (建
設項目環境保護管理條例) and other.
為了保護水源地環境，與水源地自然生態相 For the protection of the water source environment and the
和諧，本集團盡力減小對周邊環境的影響。 harmonization with the natural habitat of the water source, the Group
並且，本集團對生產及運營中的水資源、包 has made best efforts to minimize the impact on the surrounding
裝材料及能源使用情況、排污及碳排放情況 environment. In addition, the Group conducts specific monitoring
進行專門監察及管理，嚴格管控生產運營中 and management of water resources, packaging materials and
的環境生態影響。同時，本集團建立和不斷 energy usage, sewage and carbon emissions during the production
完善內部環境保護及可持續發展管理制度， and operations, and strictly controls the environmental and
通過構建良好的工廠健康、安全與環境管理 ecological impacts during the production and operations. Meanwhile,
體系及秩序，實現對工廠水資源、能源以及 the Group has established and been continuously improving the
三廢排放的全面管理，提升整體環境績效表 internal environmental protection and sustainable development
現。 management system. Through building a good health, safety and
environmental management system and order in the factory, the
overall management of the water resources, energy and three
wastes emissions in the factory will be achieved, and the overall
environmental performance will be improved.
有關本集團的環境政策及表現詳情，請參閱 For details of the Group’s environmental policies and performance,
本集團計劃於二零二二年第二季度發佈的集 please refer to the first “2021 Sustainability (ESG) Report” planned to
團《2021可持續發展報告（ESG報告）》。 be released by the Group in the second quarter of 2022.
委託存款及逾期存款 ENTRUSTED DEPOSIT AND MATURED TIME

相關披露載於綜合財務報表附註2.4、3、5、25及26。 • 審核貴集團對預期銷售獎勵折扣及銷售折扣以及
應付回扣的評估；檢查貴集團的銷售獎勵折扣歷
史數據，及將貴集團記錄的實際銷售獎勵折扣與
本年度的銷售額進行比較；
• 選擇年內銷售獎勵折扣樣本，將各選定項目與相
關銷售合約及客戶協議進行比較，重新計算貴集
團的應付銷售折扣及回扣；及
• 將銷售獎勵折扣的其後實際結算與年末相應計提
金額進行比較。
99

1. 公司及集團資料 1. CORPORATE AND GROUP INFORMATION
農夫山泉股份有限公司於2001年6月 Nongfu Spring Co., Ltd. was incorporated and registered in the
27日在中華人民共和國（「中國」）註冊 People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”) on 27 June 2001. On 8
成立及登記。於2020年9月8日，本公 September 2020, the Company was listed on the Main Board
司於香港聯合交易所有限公司（「香港 of the Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “HKSE”)
聯交所」）主板上市（股份代號：9633. (stock code: 9633. HK). The address of the registered office is
HK）。註冊辦事處地址為浙江省杭州 No. 181, Geyazhuang Road, Xihu District, Hangzhou, Zhejiang
市西湖區葛衙莊181號。 Province.
本集團參與以下主要業務： The Group was involved in the following principal activities:
－ 生產及銷售包裝飲用水及飲料 – Production and sale of packaged water and beverage
－ 銷售農產品 – Sale of agricultural products
管理層認為，本公司的控股公司及最 In the opinion of management, the holding company and the
終控股公司為養生堂有限公司，該公 ultimate holding company of the Company is Yangshengtang
司於中國註冊成立。 Co., Ltd., which is incorporated and registered in the PRC.

以及香港公司條例之披露規定。 by the International Accounting Standards Committee and
the disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong Companies
Ordinance.

本原則編製。除另有註明者外，該等 financial instruments and wealth management products which
財務報表以人民幣（「人民幣」）呈列， have been measured at fair value. These financial statements
且所有數值均調整至最近的千元單位。 are presented in Renminbi (“RMB”) and all values are rounded
to the nearest thousand except when otherwise indicated.

2.4 重大會計政策概要 2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING
POLICIES
公平值計量 Fair value measurement
本集團計量各報告期末的衍生金融工 The Group measures its derivative financial instruments and
具及股本投資。公平值為市場參與者 equity investments at the end of each reporting period. Fair
於計量日期在有序交易中出售資產所 value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or
收取或轉讓負債所支付的價格。公平 paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between
值計量乃基於假設出售資產或轉讓負 market participants at the measurement date. The fair value
債的交易於資產或負債的主要市場或 measurement is based on the presumption that the transaction
（在無主要市場的情況下）資產或負債 to sell the asset or transfer the liability takes place either in the
的最有利市場進行。主要或最有利市 principal market for the asset or liability, or in the absence of a
場必須為本集團可進入的市場。資產 principal market, in the most advantageous market for the asset
或負債的公平值乃採用市場參與者為 or liability. The principal or the most advantageous market
資產或負債定價時所用的假設計量， must be accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset
並假設市場參與者依照彼等的最佳經 or a liability is measured using the assumptions that market
濟利益行事。 participants would use when pricing the asset or liability,
assuming that market participants act in their economic best
interest.
非金融資產的公平值計量經計及市場 A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into
參與者能否以最高及最佳用途使用有 account a market participant’s ability to generate economic
關資產或能否將有關資產出售予會以 benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by
最高及最佳用途使用有關資產的另一 selling it to another market participant that would use the asset
名市場參與者以產生經濟利益。 in its highest and best use.
本集團採取適用於不同情況且具備充 The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in
分數據以供計量公平值的估值技巧， the circumstances and for which sufficient data are available to
以盡量使用相關可觀察輸入數據及盡 measure fair value, maximising the use of relevant observable
量減少使用不可觀察輸入數據。 inputs and minimising the use of unobservable inputs.
於財務報表內計量或披露公平值的所 All assets and liabilities for which fair value is measured or
有資產及負債基於對公平值計量整體 disclosed in the financial statements are categorised within the
屬重要的最低輸入數據水平，按如下 fair value hierarchy, described as follows, based on the lowest
所述在公平值層級中分類： level input that is significant to the fair value measurement as a
whole:
第一級 － 基於相同資產或負債於 Level 1 – based on quoted prices (unadjusted) in active
活躍市場的報價（未經 markets for identical assets or liabilities
調整）
第二級 － 基於可直接或間接觀察 Level 2 – based on valuation techniques for which the
對公平值計量而言屬重 lowest level input that is significant to the
大的最低層輸入數據的 fair value measurement is observable, either
估值技巧 directly or indirectly
第三級 － 基於不可觀察對公平值 Level 3 – based on valuation techniques for which the
計量而言屬重大的最低 lowest level input that is significant to the fair
層輸入數據的估值技巧 value measurement is unobservable

就按經常性基準於財務報表確認的資 For assets and liabilities that are recognised in the financial
產及負債而言，本集團透過於各報告 statements on a recurring basis, the Group determines whether
期間末重新評估分類（基於對公平值計 transfers have occurred between levels in the hierarchy by re-
量整體而言屬重大的最低層輸入數據） assessing categorisation (based on the lowest level input that is

非金融資產減值 Impairment of non-financial assets
倘有跡象顯示出現減值，或須就資產 Where an indication of impairment exists, or when annual
進行年度減值測試（存貨、遞延稅項資 impairment testing for an asset is required (other than
產、金融資產及非流動資產除外）， inventories, deferred tax assets, financial assets and non-
則會估計資產的可收回金額。資產的 current assets), the asset’s recoverable amount is estimated.
可收回金額按資產或現金產生單位的 An asset’s recoverable amount is the higher of the asset’s or
使用價值及其公平值減出售成本兩者 cash-generating unit’s value in use and its fair value less costs
中的較高者計算，並就個別資產而釐 of disposal, and is determined for an individual asset, unless

租賃－估算增量借款利率 Leases – Estimating the incremental borrowing rate
本集團無法輕易釐定租賃內所隱含的 The Group cannot readily determine the interest rate implicit in
利率，因此，使用增量借款利率（「增 a lease, and therefore, it uses an incremental borrowing rate
量借款利率」）計量租賃負債。增量借 (“IBR”) to measure lease liabilities. The IBR is the rate of interest
款利率為本集團於類似經濟環境中為 that the Group would have to pay to borrow over a similar
取得與使用權資產價值相近之資產， term, and with a similar security, the funds necessary to obtain
而以類似抵押品與類似期間借入所需 an asset of a similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar
資金應支付之利率。因此，增量借款 economic environment. The IBR therefore reflects what the
利率反映了本集團「應支付」的利率， Group “would have to pay”, which requires estimation when
當無可觀察的利率時（如就並無訂立融 no observable rates are available (such as for subsidiaries that
資交易之附屬公司而言）或當須對利率 do not enter into financing transactions) or when it needs to
進行調整以反映租賃之條款及條件時 be adjusted to reflect the terms and conditions of the lease
（如當租賃並非以附屬公司之功能貨幣 (for example, when leases are not in the subsidiary’s functional
訂立時），則須作出利率估計。當可觀 currency). The Group estimates the IBR using observable
察輸入數據可用時，本集團使用可觀 inputs (such as market interest rates) when available and is
察輸入數據（如市場利率）估算增量借 required to make certain entity-specific estimates (such as the
款利率並須作出若干實體特定的估計 subsidiary’s stand-alone credit rating).
（例如附屬公司的獨立信貸利率）。

of market inputs.

舍）的租賃期為3至5年。其他租賃協議 has lease terms between 3 and 7 years, while others including

並無重大集中的信貸風險。本集團並 diversified customers, there is no significant concentration
無就其貿易應收款項及應收票據結餘 of credit risk. The Group does not hold any collateral or

品。貿易應收款項及應收票據結餘不 balances. The balances of trade and bills receivables are non-

本集團採用簡化法計提國際財務報告 The Group applies the simplified approach to providing for
準則第9號所規定的預期信貸虧損，其 expected credit losses prescribed by IFRS 9, which permits the
允許就所有貿易應收款項使用全期預 use of the lifetime expected credit loss provision for all trade
期信貸虧損撥備。 receivables.
應收票據為獲銀行於期限內無條件接 Bills receivable are bank acceptance bills that are
納的銀行承兌票據，且並不會就應收 unconditionally accepted by banks within the maturity period,
票據減值計提虧損撥備。 and there is no loss allowance for impairment of bills receivable.
年末使用撥備矩陣進行減值分析，以 An impairment analysis is performed at year end using a
計量貿易應收款項的預期信貸虧損。 provision matrix to measure expected credit losses for trade
撥備率乃基於具有類似虧損模式的多 receivables. The provision rates are based on days past due
個客戶分部組別的逾期日數釐定。該 for groupings of various customer segments with similar loss
計算反映了概率加權結果，貨幣時間 patterns. The calculation reflects the probability-weighted
價值以及年末可獲得的關於過往事 outcome, the time value of money and reasonable and
件、當前狀況及未來經濟狀況預測的 supportable information that is available at year end about past
合理及可支持的資料。 events, current conditions and forecasts of future economic
conditions.
當有資訊表明交易對手處於嚴重財務 The Group writes off trade receivables when there is
困難時，且並無合理預期收回時，本 information indicating that the counterparty is in severe financial
集團將撤銷其貿易應收款項。本集團 difficulties and there is no realistic prospect of recovery. The
亦在合適時考慮法律意見，例如交易 Group also takes into account legal advice where appropriate,
對手已被清盤或已進入破產程序，以 e.g. when the counterparty has been placed under liquidation
較早者為準。 or has entered into bankruptcy proceedings, whichever occurs
sooner.

31. 儲備 31. RESERVES
本集團的儲備金額及其變動於財務報 The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the movements
表的綜合權益變動表呈列。 therein are presented in the consolidated statement of changes
in equity in the financial statements.
(i) 法定儲備 (i) Statutory reserve
根據中華人民共和國公司法，中 In accordance with the Company Law of the People’s
國公司須將法定除稅後利潤的 Republic of China, the companies in the PRC are
10%轉撥至法定儲備，直至儲備 required to allocate 10% of the statutory after tax profits
的累計總額達到公司註冊資本的 to the statutory reserve until the cumulative total of
50%。待中國有關部門批准後， the reserve reaches 50% of the companies’ registered
法定儲備可用於抵銷任何累計虧 capital. Subject to the approval of the relevant PRC
損或增加公司的註冊資本。法定 authorities, the statutory reserve may be used to offset
儲備不可用於向中國附屬公司股 any accumulated losses or increase the registered capital
東分派股息。 of the companies. The statutory reserve is not available
for dividend distribution to shareholders of the PRC
subsidiaries.
(ii) 資本儲備 (ii) Capital reserve
本集團的資本儲備指最終控股公 The capital reserve of the Group represents the
司及股東的貢獻及代價超出所收 contribution from the ultimate holding company and
購非控股權益的賬面值的部分。 shareholders and the excess of the consideration over
the carrying amount of the non-controlling interests
acquired.
(iii) 匯兌波動儲備 (iii) Exchange fluctuation reserve
匯兌波動儲備指因換算其功能貨 The exchange fluctuation reserve represents exchange
幣與本集團呈列貨幣不同的海外 differences arising from the translation of the financial
業務的財務報表而產生的匯兌差 statements of foreign operations whose functional
額。 currencies are different from the Group’s presentation
currency.

40. 金融風險管理目標及政策（續） 40. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT OBJECTIVES
AND POLICIES (continued)
外幣風險 Foreign currency risk
於截至2021年12月31日止年度，本集 During the year ended 31 December 2021, the Group had
團擁有以外幣計值的銀行現金，並承 cash at banks denominated in foreign currencies and was
受因各種貨幣敞口（主要為美元及港 exposed to foreign exchange risk arising from various currency
元）產生的外匯風險。外匯風險來自以 exposures, primarily with respect to USD and HKD. Foreign
美元及港元計值的現金及銀行結餘。 exchange risk arises from cash and bank balances in USD and
HKD.
於2021年及2020年12月31日，以人民 Details of long-term bank deposits and cash and bank
幣計值的長期銀行定期存款以及現金 balances denominated in RMB as at 31 December 2021 and
及銀行結餘的詳情披露於附註23。 2020 are disclosed in note 23.
下表詳述本集團對人民幣兌美元或港 The following table details the Group’s sensitivity to a 5%
元（本集團可能面臨重大風險的外幣） increase and decrease in RMB against USD or HKD, the
升值及貶值5%的敏感度。該敏感度分 foreign currency with which the Group may have a material
析僅包括以外幣計值的貨幣項目，於 exposure. The sensitivity analysis includes only outstanding
各報告期末因應外幣匯率變動5%而調 foreign currency denominated monetary items and adjusts their

資本管理 Capital management
本集團資本管理的主要目標為確保本 The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management are
集團持續經營的能力以及維持穩健的 to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern
資本比率，以支持其業務及為股東爭 and to maintain healthy capital ratios in order to support its
取最大價值。 business and maximise shareholders’ value.
本集團考慮經濟狀況變化及相關資產 The Group manages its capital structure and makes
的風險特徵來管理其資本架構並作出 adjustments to it in light of changes in economic conditions
相應調整。為維持或調整資本架構， and the risk characteristics of the underlying assets. To
本集團或會調整支付予股東的股息、 maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group may adjust
退回股本予股東或發行新股。本集團 the dividend payment to shareholders, return capital to
不受任何外部資本限制的約束。年 shareholders or issue new shares. The Group is not subject to
內，本集團的資本管理目標、政策或 any externally imposed capital requirements. No changes were
程序未發生變化。 made in the objectives, policies or processes for managing
capital during the year.
本集團採用資本負債比率監控資本， The Group monitors capital using a gearing ratio, which is debt
該比率按債務除以經調整資本計算。 divided by the adjusted capital. Debt includes interest-bearing
債務包括計息借貸以及租賃負債。資 borrowings and lease liabilities. Capital represents equity
本指母公司擁有人應佔權益。於各報 attributable to owners of the parent. The gearing ratios as at
告期末的資本負債比率如下： the end of each reporting period were as follows:

決議案以採納員工股權激勵計劃。有 employee share incentive scheme. For details, please refer to
關詳情，請參閱本公司日期為2021年 the circulars dated 24 December 2021 and the announcement
12月24日的通函及日期為2022年1月 of the Company dated 14 January 2022.
14日的公告。

勵計劃，本公司向107名本集團合資格 scheme, the Company granted awards that representing
僱員及1名養生堂人士分別授出對應本 6,596,800 and 39,600 underlying H Shares of the Company,
集團6,596,800股及39,600股H股的激 respectively, to 107 eligible employees of the Group and
勵份額，彼等對本集團之業務表現作 one individual of Yangshengtang, who have made significant
出重大貢獻，並在本集團未來發展上 contributions to the Group’s business performance, and play
擔當重要角色。 an important role in the Group’s future development.
截至2021年12月31日止年度後並無其 There was no other material subsequent event after the year
他重大期後事件。 ended 31 December 2021.
